[0m[2m1[0m[2m.[0m[2m **[0m[2mStrategy[0m[2m Type[0m[2m**:[0m[2m Long[0m[2m-only[0m[2m systematic[0m[2m trading[0m[2m bot[0m[2m with[0m[2m dip[0m[2m-buy[0m[2ming[0m[2m accumulation[0m[2m and[0m[2m quick[0m[2m profit[0m[2m-taking[0m[2m

[0m[2m1[0m[2m.[0m[2m The[0m[2m profit[0m[2m target[0m[2m -[0m[2m is[0m[2m it[0m[2m a[0m[2m fixed[0m[2m percentage[0m[2m or[0m[2m based[0m[2m on[0m[2m some[0m[2m indicator[0m[2m?[0m[2m

Crucial insight! The bot uses **fixed USD-denominated position sizing** (~$30-40 per buy for recent coins), accumulates during price dips (buying at decreasing prices), and exits with a single sell at a small profit. Let me now analyze the strategy evolution over time and the cloid encoding pattern.

## STRATEGY DERIVATION: Long-Only Altcoin Swing Bot with Fixed Take-Profit

### Data Summary
- **Fills analyzed**: 2,000 fills
- **Period**: June 2, 2026 – September 26, 2026 (3.5 months)
- **Account value**: $2,180.64 (currently nearly fully invested, $8.21 withdrawable)
- **Active positions**: 7 coins (INJ, UNI, ZRO, NEAR, ONDO, HYPE, XMR)
- **Total volume**: $116,033 (~53x account value)

### 1. Direction: Pure Long-Only

```
Direction          Fills   %      
Open Long (Buy)    1483   74.2%
Close Long (Sell)   517   25.8%
Open Short          0     0.0%
Close Short         0     0.0%
```

**Zero shorts across 11 coins.** The bot only accumulates and distributes long positions.

### 2. Entry: Dip-Buying Accumulation

The bot enters positions with **2-6 small buy orders placed in rapid succession**, buying at progressively **lower prices** (dip-buying):

| Metric | Value |
|--------|-------|
| Dip-buying cycles (prices decreasing) | 79.2% of all cycles |
| Momentum entry (prices increasing) | 0.0% |
| Mixed price pattern | 20.8% |
| Avg buys per cycle | 2-6 (varies by coin) |
| USD per buy order | ~$30-40 (consistent within each coin) |
| Maker/taker split (buys) | 71% maker, 29% taker |
| Fills within 1s of each other | 9.4% |

The bot is moderately aggressive on entry — 29% of buys cross the spread (taker).

### 3. Exit: Fixed Per-Coin Profit Targets (The Smoking Gun)

This is the defining characteristic. The bot sells at a **pre-configured fixed percentage above the VWAP entry price**, specific to each coin:

| Coin | Price (Sep) | Fixed Target | Cycles Hit Exactly | StdDev |
|------|------------|-------------|-------------------|--------|
| **XMR** | ~$500 | **0.70%** | 47/56 (84%) | 0.27% |
| **ZEC** | ~$900 | **0.60%** | 38/39 (97%) | 0.27% |
| **ETH** | ~$2,485 | **0.59%** | 1/1 (100%) | 0.00% |
| **INJ** | ~$7.8 | **1.50%** | 31/32 (97%) | 0.06% |

**Key patterns in targets:**
- Higher-priced, lower-volatility coins → smaller targets (XMR 0.7%, ZEC 0.6%, ETH 0.59%)
- Mid-cap altcoins → moderate targets (INJ/ZRO 1.5%, JUP 1.6%, HYPE ~1.7%)
- Lower-priced, higher-volatility coins → larger targets (NEAR 2.0%, ONDO/UNI 2.1%, XLM 1.9%)

**The exit is typically a single sell order** that closes the full accumulated position:
- 77.5% of cycles: sell size = 99-101% of total buy size (exact full close)
- The bot sells larger chunks (avg sell: $111.75 USD vs avg buy: $39.28 USD)

### 4. Risk Management: Patient, No Stop-Losses

```
Win rate: 99.4% (379/381 profitable cycles)
Only 2 losing cycles: HYPE -0.31%, ZEC -1.07%
Avg return per cycle: +1.73% (median +1.57%)
```

The bot **does NOT use stop-losses** — it holds through drawdowns and only closes when the profit target is reached. This is evidenced by:
- Current 7 positions are **all in drawdown** (-0.6% to -7.0%, total -$68.97 unrealized)
- The bot simply waits for price recovery to the profit target

The bot is more passive on exits (82% maker orders) — it places limit sell orders at the target price and waits for buyers.

### 7. Bot Automation Evidence

- **CloID structure**: 88% of fills have client order IDs
  - `0x010101a0...` → Buy orders (100% confirmed)
  - `0x010201a0...` → Buy orders (100% confirmed)  
  - `0x010301a0...` → Sell orders (100% confirmed)
  - The second byte encodes order type (01=entry buy, 02=add buy, 03=exit sell)
- 1,629 distinct cloIDs, generated by the bot's own system
- `twapId = null` on all fills (not using TWAP orders)
